# Open threads after Volume 06 Batch 0001 (Chapters 251 to 260)

**Additive to every section above. VOLUME 06 IS OPEN AND BLOCK 0001 IS WRITTEN. Every thread in this section is open and is handed to Batch 0002 as it stands. Nothing in this block was closed.**

## Opened in this block and live

- **A PRINTED SHEET AT A GATEPOST, ONE NUMBER AND ONE DAY, NO PLACE AND NO PERSON, AND THE NUMBER WAS ARRIVED AT BY COUNTING THE PEOPLE WHO ANSWERED A DOOR.** 251, read out and read back. **FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN, and they are to be moved on or before the first day of the month after next, which is the first day of the eighth month and is fifty-two days off at the end of this block.** **OPEN, AND IT IS THE VOLUME.**
- **THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN AND FORTY-ONE IS THREE HUNDRED AND SEVENTEEN, AND A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR SAID OUT LOUD THAT A SUBTRACTION IS ARITHMETIC AND NOT A FINDING, AND SAID THAT WHAT THE DIFFERENCE IS MADE OF IS THREE HUNDRED AND SEVENTEEN PEOPLE WHO HAVE NOT BEEN ASKED ANYTHING BY ANYBODY AT ALL.** 260. **OPEN. IT IS THE HOLE IN THE MIDDLE OF THIS MONTH AND IT IS NOT PRICED AND NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS A WAY TO PRICE IT.**
- **THE RIVAL RECORD IS IN FORCE FROM THE FIRST OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND THE DISTRICT HAS NOT ANSWERED IT AND IS NOT GOING TO.** 251, the eighth reading refused out loud, seven readings unchanged, ten days in force at the end of the block. **OPEN. A THING WITH NO DAY IN FRONT OF IT IS A THING THAT CAN BE FORGOTTEN WITHOUT ANYBODY DECIDING TO FORGET IT.**
- **THE REGISTRAR OF THIS DISTRICT'S RECORDS OFFICE HAS OPENED A SECOND BOOK IN THE ROOM WHERE THE BOOKS ARE, IN VIOLATION OF HER OWN RULE OF THE TWENTY-FIRST OF THE FOURTH MONTH, AND SAID SO OUT LOUD.** 256, 257, 259. **OPEN, AND IT IS THE VOLUME'S CENTRAL INSTRUMENT AND ITS CENTRAL VIOLATION, AND NEITHER OF THOSE IS A FINDING YET. Two open pages carried forty-one lines at four in the morning on the seventh and nobody was asked to count them.**
- **A BUILDING OF ONE ROOM AT THE NEAR END OF THE LANE BEHIND THE BANK HAS TWO DOORS, AND AN ORDER ON THREE PRINTED LINES SAYS IT IS TO BE CLOSED ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH BY ITS KEEPER, AND THE THIRD LINE PRINTS THE RULE FOR THE JOB AND AN EMPTY COLUMN FOR THE PERSON WHO HAS BEEN ASKED.** 255, 258. **OPEN, AND THE ELEVENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH IS THE FIRST DAY OF THE NEXT BLOCK.**
- **THE HEARTH BELL HANGS IN AN OAK FRAME FOUR MILES OUT PAST THE LOAMS, IS CRACKED A THIRD OF THE WAY UP ON THE SIDE THAT FACES THE LOAMS, HAS ABOUT THREE FEET OF ITS ROPE GONE AND NINE INCHES LEFT, AND HAS BEEN RUNG TWICE IN NINE YEARS.** 259. **OPEN. NOT RUNG IN THIS BLOCK AND NOT TO BE RUNG BEFORE THE VOLUME'S REVERSAL, WHICH IS A SOUNDING.**
- **THE BELL'S TWO PAST RINGINGS ARE ON TWO WALLS A MILE APART, IN TWO HANDS, AND NEITHER WALL SAYS WHO HEARD IT. The first is a single line of whitewash with the top rung gone: RUNG. THE ELEVENTH OF THE THIRD MONTH OF THE FIFTEENTH YEAR. AT NIGHT. NOBODY CAME. The second is four lines of charcoal in another hand and the last of them is rubbed under a heading that says NAMES OF WHO HEARD IT.** 259. **OPEN, AND THE RECORD THE VOLUME'S NEXT QUESTION NEEDS DOES NOT EXIST YET, AND THE HEADING ON THE RUBBED LINE IS THE PLACE IT WILL GO.**
- **A PANEL ARRIVED IN A YARD FOUR MILES OUT PAST THE LOAMS AND NOBODY DISAGREED WITH IT OUT LOUD, AND THE RECORD ABOUT THE DISAGREEING SAYS NOT ASKED.** 259. **OPEN. The disagreement happened later in a room with a shut door in it with one man in it, was not counted, and the record about it came from him and not from her. A man of fifty-six said he disagreed with the third line and did not say the rest of it.**
- **A WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO KEEPS A ROUTE FOR PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT IN ANY BOOK CAME DOWN FOUR MILES AFTER ELEVEN MONTHS, REFUSED A NAME UNDER A LINE IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS, REFUSED TO SAY HOW MANY PEOPLE THERE ARE, AND SAID THE ROUTE DOES NOT PASS THE FRAME.** 253, 259. **OPEN, AND SHE IS THE ONLY PERSON WHO CAN ANSWER THE FOURTH HUNDRED AND ELEVEN AND HAS REFUSED TWICE, AND SHE IS NOT A KEEPER OF A ROUTE AND HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED TO BE ONE.**
- **A ROOM WITH NOTHING OVER THE DOOR ABOUT TWO MILES THE OTHER WAY, AND ELEVEN NOTCHES CUT IN THE UNDERSIDE OF A BENCH THAT ARE NOT A CALENDAR AND ARE NOT A COUNT OF NIGHTS AND ARE NOT A COUNT OF PEOPLE.** 257. **OPEN, AND IF A SHEET ARRIVES IN THAT ROOM THE NOTCHES GO, AND THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE ROOM SAID SO OUT LOUD.**
- **THE FIRST QUESTION ANYBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS ASKED THE WOMAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT, AND SHE DID NOT ANSWER IT.** 254, at about six in the evening on the fourth of the sixth month, on the bank, with a bucket in each hand. The man of about thirty-four who mends fencing asked her whether the ninth of the eleven houses she carries water to is one of the four hundred and eleven on the sheet at that gatepost, in his own words and not in the six things, and she looked at him for about as long as it takes to lift a bucket and went up the bank with the empty one and did not answer him. A clerk entered that a person who has been asked a question and has not answered it has not refused it and cannot be counted either way, and that the record about the ninth of the eleven houses says not asked, and that nobody sent for him to ask it and that he is going to go on asking it and has not said so out loud. **OPEN, AND IT COSTS HIM SOMETHING EVERY DAY HE ASKS IT, AND IT IS THE QUESTION THE WHOLE BLOCK IS ABOUT ASKED IN A PLACE WHERE THE ASKING IS VISIBLE.** A man of fifty-six said one uncounted sentence about it and the record about that sentence came from the man of about twenty-nine who drives a cart and is not a man of about nineteen who counts. **IT MAY BE ANSWERED BY NOBODY WHO HAS NOT BEEN ASKED, AND IT MAY NOT BE ANSWERED BY THE REGISTRAR OUT OF A BOOK, WHICH WOULD BE THE FIFTH THING THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE.**
- **A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO DRIVES A CART.** 254, new in this block, and he is a person doing plot work and not atmosphere: he proposed walking the ground in the shadow of the Hall, counting the doors and putting the number in chalk on a board, and the man of about thirty-four who digs loam refused it and a clerk entered that the four columns of not-askings are four and she did not rule one. **HE IS NOT THE WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO KEEPS THE ROOM WITH NOTHING OVER THE DOOR, the two are about the same age in two trades, and they are not merged and the trade is the identifier and it never carries the identification alone.** Open, and a Batch 0002 writer is not to let the age stand in for either of them.
- **A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-FOUR CAME DOWN THE ELEVEN MILE ROAD IN THE SECOND WEEK OF LAST MONTH WITH A CASE, AND DID NOT COME INTO THE YARD, AND DID NOT COME BACK.** 257. **OPEN, AND THE BROKER WHO OFFERS TO BROKER IDENTITIES FOR PROFIT IS NOT YET ON ANY PAGE AND IS BATCH 0002'S.**
- **THE ROMANCE MOVED TWICE IN TEN DAYS, IN A DOORWAY AND IN A ROOM WITH A SHUT DOOR IN IT, AND A SLATE WENT PAST THAT KITCHEN FOR THE THIRTEENTH TIME AND CAME BACK WITH ONE LINE ON IT ABOUT FORTY-ONE.** 256, 259, 260. **OPEN. NEITHER MOVEMENT IS A DECLARATION. He said he was not going to ask her what was on it and she said do not come and find me about it, and neither of them said another word, and the record came from him and not from her.**
- **HE SAID OUT LOUD IN A YARD THAT A DOOR IS A THING YOU BUILD FOR THE PEOPLE WHO COME TO IT, AND THAT HE HAS BUILT NINE, AND THAT THE ONLY PEOPLE WHO HAVE COME TO THEM ARE THE ONES WHO ALREADY HAD A REASON TO COME. A MAN OF FIFTY-SIX TOLD HIM THAT STANDING NEXT TO A THING IS NOT STANDING IN ITS PLACE.** 260. **OPEN. IT IS THE VOLUME'S POWER CHANGE AND IT IS NOT SPENT, AND THE LESSON HAS BEEN STATED ONCE AND NOT ACTED ON.**
- **A DOCUMENT THAT NAMES A JOB AND A RULE FOR FILLING IT AND AN EMPTY COLUMN FOR THE PERSON WHO HAS BEEN ASKED IS A WORKING DEMONSTRATION OF WHAT A MARK CAN DO TO A SHEET, AND IT ARRIVED FORTY-ONE DAYS BEFORE THE ENDING'S FOUNDER'S MARK DOES THE SAME THING TO A RECORD, AND NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS CONNECTED THOSE TWO THINGS AND NOBODY IS TOO.** 258. **OPEN.**
- **THE NINTH OF THE NINE NIGHTS.** The ninth was the twentieth of the fourth month. 251 at forty-two days back, 260 at fifty-one. **OPEN AND NOT TO BE CLOSED BY ANYBODY. A plank does not keep nights.**
- **THE FIFTH OF THE FIVE THINGS THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE IS A WAY TO PAY A PERSON WHO IS NOT IN A HOUSEHOLD, AND IT WAS NAMED OUT LOUD IN A ROOM WITH NOTHING OVER THE DOOR AND REFUSED BEFORE THE PERSON WHO KEEPS THAT ROOM WAS ASKED, ON THE GROUND THAT IF THE FIFTH WERE ALSO THE FIFTH OF THAT ROOM SOMEBODY WOULD COME AND ASK HER WHAT SHE WOULD TAKE FOR IT.** 257. **OPEN, AND IT IS THE EXACT HOLE THE BROKER'S OFFER IS SHAPED TO FIT, AND IT HAS NOW BEEN REFUSED EIGHT TIMES IN A MONTH BY THIS DISTRICT.**

## Corrections this block made, that a Batch 0002 writer must inherit

- **THE RELATIVE MONTH TURNED ITS MEANING ON THE FIRST OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND IT CHANGES IN WRITING.** From the first of the sixth month, *last month* means the FIFTH month and *the month before last* means the FOURTH, and the third month is two months back and must be named that way or by its own name. **THE POOL OF REFUSALS WITH NO REASON LAST MOVED ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE THIRD MONTH AND IS THEREFORE TWO MONTHS BACK AND SIXTY-NINE DAYS, AND THE PHRASE *LAST MONTH* MUST NOT BE USED FOR IT.** **SIX RELATIVE-MONTH PHRASES WERE WRONG IN THIS BLOCK'S FIRST DRAFT AND ALL SIX WERE CAUGHT BY READING EACH ONE AGAINST THE MONTH IT NAMES. THAT IS THE CHECK AND IT IS THE ONLY ONE THAT WORKS.**
- **ALL SEVEN MONTH LENGTHS ARE NOW STATED IN ONE PLACE. The first has thirty-one, the second had twenty-eight, the third has thirty-one, the fourth has thirty, the fifth has thirty-one, the sixth has thirty, and the seventh has thirty-one. NO FIGURE IN VOLUME 06 MAY USE TWENTY-NINE OR THIRTY-ONE FOR THE SIXTH MONTH. THE LENGTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH IS NOT STATED IN THIS CANON, NO CHAPTER OF THIS VOLUME STANDS ON A DAY OF IT, AND NO FIGURE IN THE VOLUME MAY USE ONE — the first of the eighth month is a day the volume counts forward to and nothing more. The thirty-one days of the seventh month are what carry the first of the eighth month sixty-one days off the first of the sixth month, and they belong to the seventh month and not to the eighth, which is the error the review repair corrected in the Batch 0001 canon card.**
- **NO CHAPTER IN CHAPTERS 251 TO 260 CARRIES TWO DATES — AND THE FIRST DRAFT OF CHAPTER 257 DID.** Ten days, ten chapters, one a day. **The claim stood as verified in the first pass of the block record and it was false: Chapter 257 closed its last scene on *the next afternoon*, which is the eighth's afternoon, in a chapter dated the seventh, and the sequence ran backwards into Chapter 258's opening. The review pulled the scene back onto the seventh's own afternoon, where its figure of forty-seven days is the seventh's figure, and the claim is now true of the prose.** **NO WEEKDAY NAME IS SPOKEN IN ANY OF THE TEN AND NO WEEKDAY IS ATTACHED TO ANY DATE.** This is the fifth consecutive clean block.
- **NO PLACE IS NAMED ON ANY DOCUMENT IN THIS DISTRICT AND NONE IS NAMED IN NARRATION.** A scan for *Alder Reach*, *Verdict*, *Brass*, *Morrow*, *Uplands*, *Crownless* and every person-name in the series' cast returns zero.
- **A KEEPER HAS NEVER BEEN ON A PAGE AND THERE NEVER WAS ONE AND NO KEEPER IS NAMED.** **The bare word occurs TWENTY-ONE TIMES in this block, against three in the block before it, one per 1,196 words against one per 8,523 — the largest relative movement of any frame in the block and the volume's most guarded noun. The narrower literal string *a keeper* is thirteen, and the first pass of the block record printed thirteen as the figure for the word, which was the narrower string and not the word; both are now in the frame table. Every one of the twenty-one is a denial, a rule printed on a sheet, a column that is empty, or a noun somebody has been told they are not.**
- **A REVIEW MUST RUN SIX CHECKS AND ALL SIX ARE NAMED IN THE BLOCK RECORD AT SECTION 4:** (1) a class-one and class-two counting-motif detector **calibrated on Chapters 241 to 250 first**, which returns 53 claims, 0 mismatches, a denominator of 25,569, a `wc -w` total of 25,689 and 675 shared twelve-word runs and all five reproduce; (2) a calendar check, field by field, against the derivation, **plus a separate read of every relative-month phrase against the month it names**, because the ladder can be right and the phrases around it can be wrong, and six were. **AND A SEVENTH CHECK THE REVIEW ADDED AFTER THE SIXTH ONE FAILED IN ITS OWN BLOCK: walk every interval out of the seven month lengths in a scratch calculation, and do not add the last known figure to the number of days since. This block's field-by-field check printed *all thirty fields are correct* and missed the removal day, which its own prose carried one day out, and a second sweep missed the same figure and then missed a second interval in the Batch 0002 prompt. Three intervals in three files, all the same mistake, all found by counting and not by reading a list.** (3) a footer-support check that searches the body of its own chapter for every number word in the footer, which returns zero; (4) a whole-word reserved-term scan run **after the last edit**; (5) a frame table with its method and its denominators printed beside it, **and the two frames the tool's printed list does not carry measured by hand with the same method and the method named, and the disclosure that the block's own instrument phrase rose for a second block running while the short form fell to zero while *not asked* and the bare word *keeper* — the two largest frames in the block — sat in the table and were not named until the review named them**; and (6) a duplicated-paragraph check, **which returned one shared paragraph of twelve words or more in this block and one in the known block, and the known block's own record claims zero, so the measure does not reproduce the earlier record in either block and that is disclosed.**
- **THE BLOCK'S OWN INSTRUMENT PHRASE HAS NOW RISEN IN TWO BLOCKS RUNNING AND THE SHORT FORM HAS FALLEN TO ZERO IN THIS ONE — AND THE TWO LARGEST FRAMES IN THE BLOCK WERE LEFT IN A TABLE AND OUT OF THE READ OF IT, WHICH THE REVIEW CAUGHT.** *not asked* is seventy-seven against seventy-six and one per 327 words and it is in every one of the ten chapters. The bare word *keeper* is twenty-one against three. *entered that* is a hundred and thirty-nine against a hundred and seven and is the same sentence-initial habit wearing a different noun. **A frame that is measured has to be named in the read of the measurement, and that is now a standing item in the Batch 0002 prompt.** *clerk of nineteen years* is eighty-three against sixty-seven and one per 304 words against one per 381; *A clerk of nineteen years entered* is seventy-four against fifty-eight; *the clerk entered that* is zero against ten. **A Batch 0002 record that does not say this is reporting the half of the table that agreed.**

- **A BUILDING OF ONE ROOM AT THE NEAR END OF THE LANE BEHIND THE BANK WAS TO BE SHUT ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH BY ITS KEEPER AND WAS NOT SHUT, AND THE COLUMN FOR THE KEEPER WAS EMPTY ON THE FACE OF THE SHEET.** 261. **OPEN. - **FORTY-ONE NAMES WERE SAID OUT LOUD IN A YARD A STRANGER CAN WALK INTO ON THE SEVENTEENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH, AND THE FIGURE IS FORTY PEOPLE.** 267, 268. **OPEN, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN THIS VOLUME THAT A NAME HAS COME OUT OF THAT ROOM, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME A NAME HAS COME OUT OF A YARD IN FIVE MONTHS OF ASKING.** A man of about thirty-four who digs loam has said out loud that the arithmetic was right twice in eleven days and the figure was wrong twice, and that a man who gets a number right twice by counting lines has been counting lines, **and that a line with a person's name on it is not a person and the difference is a quarter of an inch of space at a page break.**
- **A PANEL ARRIVED IN A YARD ON THE SIXTEENTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND ONE PERSON DISAGREED WITH IT OUT LOUD AND FOUR PEOPLE SAID NOTHING.** 266. **OPEN. - **THE QUESTION ABOUT THE NINTH OF THE ELEVEN HOUSES WAS ASKED FIVE TIMES AND ANSWERED ON THE FIFTH, AND THE ANSWER IS THAT THE SHEET IS NOT A LIE.** 269. **OPEN, AND IT IS THE FIRST THING SAID IN THIS DISTRICT ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN BY A PERSON WHO WAS ASKED, AND THE NINTH OF THE ELEVEN IS THE HOUSE THE WOMAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SIX WHO KEEPS A SCALE NAMED OUT LOUD ON THE THIRD OF THE SIXTH MONTH WITHOUT BEING ASKED, AND A THING SAID BY A PERSON WHO WAS NOT ASKED AND A THING SAID BY A PERSON WHO WAS ASKED ARE TWO DIFFERENT KINDS OF THING AND ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER. No number on the sheet moved. The difference between four hundred and eleven and three hundred and seventy was not re-derived and was not priced.**
- **THE ROMANCE MOVED TWICE IN TEN DAYS, BOTH TIMES IN A LANE, AND THE HARDEST MOVEMENT OF THE VOLUME SO FAR IS ONE OF THEM REFUSING HELP HE OFFERS IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE.** 263, 268, 270. **OPEN, AND NEITHER MOVEMENT IS A DECLARATION.** The answer to the refusal is that there is no room in this district that nobody can walk into, and that answer is the exit clause the climax will have to be and is not solved. A slate went past a kitchen for the fourteenth time on the twentieth.
- **HE HAS SAID ON A SLATE THAT ON THE TWENTY-FIRST OF THE SIXTH MONTH HE IS GOING TO ASK A MAN TO STAND IN A DOORWAY, THAT THE MAN WILL SAY NO, AND THAT HE IS GOING TO ASK HIM TWICE.** 270. **OPEN. - **A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE WHO COPIES FOR NOTHING HAS SAID YES TO ELEVEN COPPERS A WEEK IN ONE SECOND AND BEEN FINISHED IN FOUR SECONDS, AND HIS HAND DOES NOT CLOSE.** 263, 268, 270. **OPEN. # Volume 06 Batch 0003 — Open threads, Chapters 271 to 280

**Additive to every section above. Volume 06 is open. Batch 0004 inherits these.**

## Opened in this block and live

- **THE BELL WAS RUNG ONCE, ON THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH, AT ABOUT TWO IN THE MORNING, FOR ABOUT NINE MINUTES, BY A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO MENDS FENCING STANDING UNDER AN OAK FRAME FOUR MILES OUT PAST THE LOAMS WITH ABOUT NINE FEET OF ROPE IN BOTH HANDS AND THE SKIN GONE OFF THE INSIDE OF HIS LEFT FOREARM.** 273, 274. **OPEN. IT IS THE FIRST SOUNDING IN NINE YEARS AND THE FIRST IN EIGHTEEN, AND THE ONLY ONE IN THIS VOLUME, AND IT IS NOT TO BE RUNG AGAIN. EVERYBODY WHO CAME OUT OF THE LANE BEHIND THE BANK WAS INSIDE A BUILDING WITH TWO DOORS BY ABOUT NINE MINUTES AFTER IT STARTED, AND THE FIGURE OF NINE MINUTES WAS GIVEN BY THE MAN STANDING STILL IN THE DARK WITH A HAND THAT DOES NOT CLOSE, AND A CLERK ENTERED THAT A FIGURE ABOUT NINE MINUTES GIVEN BY THAT MAN IS A FIGURE ABOUT NINE MINUTES AND IS NOT A FIGURE ABOUT THIS DISTRICT.**
- **A PIECE OF PAPER ABOUT AS WIDE AS A PAGE, FOLDED IN FOUR, WITH ABOUT NINE NAMES ON IT IN NINE DIFFERENT HANDS, IS IN THE FIRST OF THE TWO BOOKS IN THE ROOM WHERE THE BOOKS ARE.** 275, 278. **OPEN. ## Corrections this block made, that a Batch 0004 writer must inherit

- **THE RELATIVE MONTHS ALL TURN ON THE FIRST DAY OF THE SEVENTH MONTH, WHICH IS THE FIRST DAY OF THE NEXT BLOCK.** From the first of the seventh, *last month* is the sixth and *the month before last* is the fifth, and the third month is three months back and must be named that way or by its own name. **THE POOL OF REFUSALS WITH NO REASON, WHICH LAST MOVED ON THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE THIRD MONTH, IS THREE MONTHS BACK AND NINETY-NINE DAYS OFF THE FIRST OF THE SEVENTH.** **EVERY RELATIVE-MONTH PHRASE MUST BE READ AGAINST THE MONTH IT NAMES, SEPARATELY FROM CHECKING ANY LADDER, BECAUSE THE LADDER CAN BE RIGHT AND THE PHRASES AROUND IT CAN BE WRONG, AND FOUR WERE WRONG IN BATCH 0003'S FIRST DRAFT AND ARE CORRECTED — ONE OF THEM A COUNT OF DAYS THAT NAMED ITS OWN START IN A RELATIVE PHRASE, WHOSE LADDER WAS INTERNALLY CONSISTENT IN THE OTHER NINE CHAPTERS.**
- **CHAPTERS 271 TO 280 ARE THE TWENTY-FIRST TO THE THIRTIETH OF THE SIXTH MONTH AND DID NOT CROSS INTO THE SEVENTH, AND CHAPTERS 281 TO 290 ARE THEREFORE THE FIRST TO THE ELEVENTH OF THE SEVENTH.** The removal-day ladder falls by one every day of this block and falls by one more on the morning the month turns, and the derivation is no days to the end of the sixth month plus the thirty-one of the seventh plus the first of the eighth. **The sixth month has thirty days, the fifth thirty-one, the fourth thirty, the third thirty-one, the first thirty-one, the seventh thirty-one, and the second had twenty-eight. NO FIGURE MAY USE TWENTY-NINE OR THIRTY-ONE FOR THE SIXTH MONTH, AND THE LENGTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH IS NOT STATED IN THIS CANON AND MUST NOT BE USED — and Chapters 271 and 280 of this block both say so on the page.**
- **NO CHAPTER IN CHAPTERS 271 TO 280 CARRIES TWO DATES.** Ten days, ten chapters, one a day. **No weekday name is spoken in any of the ten, and one was written into the first draft and was found by the scan and removed before the block was measured. This is the seventh consecutive clean block after two repairs.**
- **NO PLACE IS NAMED ON ANY DOCUMENT IN THIS DISTRICT AND NONE IS NAMED IN NARRATION.** A scan for *Alder Reach*, *Verdict*, *Brass*, *Morrow*, *Uplands*, *Crownless* and every person-name in the series' cast returns zero. **The Hall is named once in narration, in Chapter 279, as a building, and the country above the water is not named.**
- **A KEEPER HAS NEVER BEEN ON A PAGE AND THERE NEVER WAS ONE AND NO KEEPER IS NAMED.** **The bare word occurs THIRTY-TWO TIMES in this block against NINETEEN in the one before it and TWENTY-ONE in the one before that, one per 835 words against one per 1,264 and one per 1,201, and it rose, and every one of the thirty-two is a denial, a printed rule about a job, a column that is empty, or a noun somebody has been told they are not.** The narrower literal string *a keeper* is twenty-nine against ten and thirteen.
- **A REVIEW MUST RUN SEVEN CHECKS AND ALL SEVEN ARE NAMED IN THE BLOCK RECORD AT SECTION 4:** (1) a class-one and class-two counting-motif detector **calibrated on Chapters 241 to 250 first**, which returns 53 claims, 0 mismatches, a denominator of 25,569, a `wc -w` total of 25,689 and 675 shared twelve-word runs and all five reproduce; (2) a calendar check, field by field, **with every interval walked out of the seven month lengths in a scratch calculation before it is written down and NOT built by adding the last known figure to the number of days since**; (3) **a separate read of every relative-month phrase against the month it names**; (4) a weekday scan, **which caught one weekday name in this block's first draft on its first run**; (5) a footer-support check that searches the body of its own chapter for every number word in the footer, which returns zero **and is not sufficient on its own, because a footer sentence can use only number words that are all in the body and still contradict the chapter, and one such sentence was found in this block by reading rather than by the pass**; (6) a whole-word reserved-term scan run **after the last edit**; (7) a frame table with its method and its denominators printed beside it, **with the frames the tool's printed list does not carry measured by hand with the same method and the method named, and the requirement that a frame measured must be named in the read of the measurement, and the ranking by count printed with it, which is the standing item the Batch 0001 review left and which Batch 0002 upheld and which this block honours and which withdrew the Batch 0002 record's wrong claim a second time**; and a duplicated-paragraph check and a duplicated-sentence check, **which both return one in this block, in the block before it and in the block before that, and it is the same sentence, and that is disclosed as a limit of the earlier records' method.**
- **THE BLOCK'S OWN INSTRUMENT PHRASE HAS NOW RISEN IN FOUR BLOCKS RUNNING AND THE RECORD NAMES IT.** *Clerk of nineteen years* is a hundred and seven against ninety-two and one per 250 words; *A clerk of nineteen years entered* is a hundred against eighty-six; *the clerk entered that* is **zero for the third block running**; *entered that* is a hundred and thirty-four against a hundred and twenty-seven. **A BATCH 0004 RECORD THAT DOES NOT SAY THIS IS REPORTING THE HALF OF THE TABLE THAT AGREED.** The bare word *keeper* rose from nineteen to thirty-one, *a bell* rose from zero to thirty-eight, *a lot* rose from zero to twenty-four, *a child* rose from zero to seventeen, *a doorway* rose from four to twenty-three and *a charter* rose from zero to nine, **and the two that fell hard are *a slate* from fourteen to four and *a second book* from three to zero, and the reason for each is on the page.**
- **THE VOLUME'S MIDPOINT REVERSAL IS SPENT AND NOTHING OF IT MAY BE SPENT AGAIN.** The bell was rung once and is not to be rung again, the child is the cost and is not to be used again as a device, the fifth line of the charter is not to be corrected, the nine names on the piece of paper are not to be printed, and the wrong sheet is the note this block ends on and NOT the note the next block opens with.
- **A REVIEW PASS FOLLOWED THIS BLOCK AND FOUND SEVENTEEN DEFECTS THAT NO COUNT COULD SEE, AND A BATCH 0004 WRITER SHOULD INHERIT THE CLASS AND NOT THE LIST.** All seventeen are named with the file, the line and the reason in `state/volume-06-batch-0003-summary.md` at section 5A. **THE CLASS IS: A SENTENCE THAT NAMES A PERSON, A PLACE, A TIME, A MEASURE OR AN EVENT HAS TO BE CHECKED AGAINST EVERY OTHER PLACE THAT NAMES IT, AND NOT AGAINST A LADDER, A DAY-COUNT OR A FIGURE, ALL OF WHICH WERE CORRECT THROUGHOUT.** Four of the seventeen were in the canon card and not in the prose, and three of those were relative months, **so the card is not a safe document at the end of a block and has to be read back against the chapters.** Two of the seventeen were a duplicated shape — the same lead-in sentence in ten consecutive chapters, and nine hundred yards of lane where the lane is two hundred — and one was a scene that no state file had ever recorded.
- **THE TEN LEAD-IN SENTENCES OF THIS BLOCK ARE NOT THE SAME SENTENCE TEN TIMES, AND THE IDENTICAL-PARAGRAPH COUNT FOR IT IS ZERO, WHICH IS THE FIRST TIME IN VOLUME 06.** **A BATCH 0004 WRITER WHO PRINTS THE OLD SENTENCE ONCE IS FINE AND ONE WHO PRINTS IT TWICE HAS PUT AN IDENTICAL PARAGRAPH INTO A BLOCK THAT HAS JUST HAD ONE REMOVED FROM IT, AND THE RECORD SHOULD SAY SO EITHER WAY.**
- **THE FIRST FIGURE IN THE WHOLE MANUSCRIPT THAT WENT DOWN ON A FIX IS IN THIS BLOCK, AND IT IS A WORD COUNT: 1,056 shared twelve-word runs against 1,038, and 26,854 words against 26,532, and thirty-two class-one claims of which four moved, from 262 to 263, from 172 to 169, from 176 to 193 and from 175 to 185.** **NONE OF THE FOUR MOVED TO FIT A SENTENCE. ALL FOUR MOVED BECAUSE A MISATTRIBUTED QUOTATION, A ROPE MEASURED AT A THIRD OF ITS LENGTH, A MAN TAKING CREDIT FOR ANOTHER MAN'S ARGUMENT AND AN INVENTED SCENE WERE CUT OUT OF THE SENTENCES THEY SIT ON, AND EVERY ONE WAS RE-MEASURED ON THE PRINTED WORDS.**

Nobody has been asked whether anybody will. **A LATER BLOCK THAT MOVES IT FROM THE STEP INTO THE BOOK HAS SPENT SOMETHING, AND A LATER BLOCK THAT MOVES IT FROM THE STEP INTO A PUBLIC PLACE HAS SPENT SOMETHING ELSE, AND A READER WHO FINDS THE SHEET IN A DIFFERENT PLACE ON A DAY A LATER CHAPTER DOES NOT NAME IS READING A DEFECT AND NOT A SURPRISE.**

## The three structural findings of the review of this block, and the two of them that need a decision and not a patch

**A REVIEW OF CHAPTERS 311 TO 320 NAMED TEN DEFECTS AND THREE STRUCTURAL CONCERNS. SEVEN OF THE DEFECTS WERE PROSE OR MEASUREMENT FAULTS AND ALL SEVEN ARE REPAIRED AND ACCOUNTED FOR AT `state/volume-07-batch-0002-summary.md` SECTION 5.1. THE THREE STRUCTURAL CONCERNS ARE NOT FAULTS IN THIS BLOCK'S SENTENCES. THEY ARE FAULTS IN WHAT THE LAST FOUR VOLUMES HAVE LEFT ON THE PAGE, THEY CANNOT BE REPAIRED INSIDE TEN CHAPTERS WITHOUT RESTARTING THEM, AND THEY ARE DISCLOSED HERE AS PROPOSED CANON CHANGES THAT A PHASE PROMPT OR A VOLUME OUTLINE HAS TO SETTLE. NONE OF THEM IS CLOSED BY THIS BLOCK AND NONE OF THEM IS CLOSED BY THE REPAIR.**

1. **THE PROTAGONIST IS NOT IN THIS BOOK ANY MORE, AND THE MEASUREMENT IS NOT A MATTER OF TASTE.** `bible/characters.md` names **Adrian Vale** as the protagonist. The string *Adrian*, case-insensitive, by volume: **Volume 01 five hundred and seventy, Volume 02 ninety, Volume 03 sixteen, Volume 04 one, Volume 05 zero, Volume 06 zero, Volume 07 zero.** He is in Chapters 311 to 320 as *the man the figure of twenty-one years is against*, in three of the ten, and he is given no interiority, no flaw, no habit, no named want and no name; he asks a question and then says out loud that he will not ask another. **THE READER HAS HAD A HUNDRED AND TWENTY CHAPTERS WITHOUT THE MAN THE BOOK IS CALLED AFTER BEING ABLE TO POINT AT.** AGENTS.md requires a male lead with a believable inner life, flaws, habits, work, relationships and changing responsibilities.

2. **THE SAME FALL HAS HAPPENED TO THE ROMANCE.** *Mara* by volume: **Volume 01 four hundred and thirty-nine, Volume 02 twenty-two, Volume 03 zero, Volume 04 zero, Volume 05 zero, Volume 06 zero, Volume 07 zero**, and `state/current.md` now carries the sentence *and in Volume 07 Batch 0002 the romance did not move, again*. **SHE IS IN NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 07 AND HE IS IN THREE, AND THE THING THE NEXT BLOCK WRITES IS THE CHARTER, WHICH IS THE FIRST PUBLIC DOCUMENT IN THIS VOLUME, AND IT IS WRITTEN BY A MAN WITH NO NAME IN THE MANUSCRIPT AND NO INTERIOR LIFE ON THE PAGE.**

3. **THE POWER SYSTEM IS NOT ON THE PAGE.** **The capitalised string *System* is at ZERO across all three hundred and twenty chapters, and *Auction* is at three, all of them in Volume 01.** `bible/power-system.md` defines a Tally with four modes, a three-reading structure and seven named power stages, and none of it is on the page. Capping panels at one a chapter and two a block is defensible; **capping them at zero for four volumes while the book is named for what its lead does is not, and the cap is not the thing that needs deciding — the silence is.**

4. **AND THE OBSERVATION BESIDE THEM, WHICH IS A MEASUREMENT AND NOT A COMPLAINT.** All ten chapters open on the same construction and close with an all-caps restatement that repeats the chapter. *cannot be counted either way* is thirty-one times across the block and six times in Chapter 319 alone. *entered that* runs once in 204 words and *clerk* once in 158. AGENTS.md names *counted* and *wrote* as filler words, and in this block they carry the load rather than filling. **The block discloses its own shared twelve-word run honestly, so this is disclosed and not hidden, and it is at the edge of the quality gate rather than past it.**

**AND WHAT IS PROPOSED, WHICH IS A CANON CHANGE AND NOT A PROSE REPAIR, AND WHICH NO PHASE HAS SETTLED YET.**

- **THE RESERVED RULE THAT HAS TO BE READ AND AMENDED IS AT `outline/volume-07.md`, IN THE RESERVED LIST: *NO PERSON IS NAMED IN NARRATION, AND NO PERSON IS NAMED ON ANY DOCUMENT IN THIS DISTRICT EXCEPT WHERE A DOCUMENT IS SOMEBODY'S OWN NAME ON THEIR OWN LINE* AND *NO NEW NAME APPEARS IN THIS VOLUME FOR ANYBODY ELSE*.** **THE RULE AS WRITTEN FORBIDS A NEW NAME. IT DOES NOT FORBID THE NAME THE READER HAS HAD SINCE CHAPTER ONE.** **THE PROTAGONIST'S NAME IS NOT A WITHHELD NAME IN THIS VOLUME. THE WITHHELD NAMES ARE THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR'S, THE NAME SAID ALOUD AT CHAPTER 336, AND EVERY NAME ON A LIST IN A BUILDING TWO DAYS OF ROAD AWAY, AND THE RULE EXISTS TO PROTECT THOSE THREE AND NOT THE AUTHOR.** **THE RECOMMENDATION IS THAT THE RULE BE AMENDED TO SAY SO EXPLICITLY, THAT THE PROTAGONIST BE NAMED IN NARRATION FROM WHATEVER CHAPTER THE AMENDMENT LANDS, AND THAT THE AMENDMENT BE MADE IN `outline/volume-07.md` AND NOT IN A CHAPTER, BECAUSE A CHAPTER THAT BREAKS A RESERVED RULE WITHOUT THE RULE BEING AMENDED IS A DEFECT AND NOT A DECISION.**
- **THE COMPANION CHANGE IS THE PROTAGONIST'S INTERIOR LIFE, WHICH IS NOT A NAMING QUESTION AT ALL AND IS THE URGENT ONE. A CHARACTER WHO IS GIVEN HIS NAME BACK AND NO THOUGHT, NO FLAW AND NO COST IS STILL NOT ON THE PAGE. HIS BLOCK HAS TO CARRY WHAT HE WANTS, WHAT HE IS WRONG ABOUT, WHAT HE DOES WITH HIS HANDS AND WHAT IT COSTS HIM, IN NARRATION AND NOT IN A FOOTER, AND THAT IS TRUE WHATEVER IS DECIDED ABOUT HIS NAME.**
- **THE THIRD CHANGE IS THE TALLY, AND THE PROPOSAL IS NOT *PUT THE POWER BACK*; IT IS THAT THE NEXT BLOCK SPEND THE ONE DOCUMENT THAT IS ALREADY ON THE PAGE AND ALREADY NAMES A BOOK — THE NEW SHEET'S *SECOND BOOK OF THE ROOM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THE GATE* — AS THE PLACE WHERE A FIGURE IS TO BE ENTERED BY A PERSON, AND THAT THE COST OF DOING IT BE THAT COMPLETING IT IS SOMEBODY'S WORK AND NOT THE AUTHOR'S. A PANEL THAT ARRIVES UNASKED AND IS THEN ARGUED WITH IS THE SHAPE THE SYSTEM HAS ALWAYS HAD. A PANEL THAT DECIDES ANYTHING IS FORBIDDEN AND REMAINS FORBIDDEN.**
- **NONE OF THE THREE IS SETTLED BY THE REPAIR OF THIS BLOCK, AND NONE OF THEM CHANGES THE PLAN.** The charter is at Chapters 321 to 330 and stays there. The second pulling is at Chapter 333 and stays there. The power change is at Chapter 334 and stays there. The name is spent in Chapters 335 to 337 and stays there. **THE REPAIR MOVED THREE SENTENCES AND TWO MEASUREMENTS. IT DID NOT MOVE A SINGLE CHAPTER CARD, A SINGLE DATE, OR A SINGLE PROMISED REVERSAL, AND IT IS NOT ENTITLED TO.**

SPENT: the woman of about thirty-one's eleven words, given once, in a room, to one person she chose out of three, not written down, not repeated, not asked for again.** **NOT SPENT: the removal did not happen on the first of the eighth month, and that day is Chapter 312 of Batch 0002 and is nine days behind Chapter 321 and eighteen days behind Chapter 330, and this block does not go back to it and does not answer it. NOT SPENT: the power change, which has been stated eleven times as a lesson and is not spent as a stage here and is not spent at all, and is at Chapter 334.** The five unmet promises are disclosed in thirteen places and the list is in the block record and at the head of the canon card.

## What this block opened, and what a later block owes it

IT IS NOT YET READ OUT AND READ BACK ON ANY DAY THAT A STRANGER COULD HAVE STOOD IN — IT IS READ OUT ONCE, ON THE EIGHTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH, IN A YARD A STRANGER CAN WALK INTO — AND IT IS NOT A FOURTH OF THE THREE DOCUMENTS NOBODY OWNS AND IT IS NOT A SEVENTH OF THE SIX INSTRUMENTS.** A later block that puts a name in that column has broken the instrument, and a later block that fills the column ruled over NOT ASKED has broken the volume.

3. **A QUESTION WAS ASKED AT A TABLE WITH FOUR LEGS OUTSIDE A DOOR AND IT WAS REFUSED, AND THE TABLE IS A COUNTER.** **THE MAN WHO ASKED IT SAID OUT LOUD IN ABOUT NINE FEET OF LANE THAT HE WAS WRONG ABOUT THE BUILDING AND DID NOT GO ROUND THE MAN, AND THE MAN WHO WAS ASKED SAID NO IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A REASON OF HIS OWN: HE HAS NO BOOTS THAT WILL GO OVER A DITCH ABOUT THREE FEET DEEP WITH ABOUT A FOOT OF STANDING WATER IN IT. THE ANSWER IS A RULE AND IT IS THE FIRST RULE IN THIS BLOCK THAT NOBODY HAD TO BE ASKED TO LEARN.** The other question was asked at a table with four legs outside a door, and it was refused. **ONE OF THE THINGS THE YARD IS GOING TO ASK IS A PERSON STANDING NEXT TO A BELL WHETHER THEY HEARD IT, AND THE ANSWER IS NOT REQUIRED AND IS NOT TAKEN, AND THAT IS STILL A LATER BLOCK'S WORK, AND ON THE FIFTEENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH NOBODY IN THAT YARD ASKED THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR ONE QUESTION ABOUT THE NIGHT OF THE TWENTY-FOURTH OF THE SIXTH MONTH.**
8. **THE HAND OF THE REGISTRAR OF THIS DISTRICT'S RECORDS OFFICE HAS NOT CLOSED PROPERLY SINCE THE TENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH.** **IT IS NOT THE FENCER'S LEFT HAND, NOT THE HAND OF THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE WHO COPIES FOR NOTHING, AND NOT THE ARM OF THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-FOUR WHO DIGS LOAM, AND A CLERK ENTERED ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH THAT FOUR HANDS AND AN ARM IN THIS DISTRICT ARE FIVE DIFFERENT THINGS AND ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER.** It is a new figure in this manuscript and it is the only temporary one: he wrote five hours, then could not hold a pen, then wrote in his left hand, then could not write at all, then could not lift a sheet, then signed in chalk with the other hand. **A LATER BLOCK MAY FIND IT BETTER AND MAY NOT FIND IT WORSE WITHOUT A SCENE, AND IT IS A FIGURE ABOUT A HAND AND NOT ABOUT A DOCUMENT.**

1. **THE SECOND PULLING IS SPENT AND IT CANNOT BE PULLED A THIRD TIME IN THIS CANON.** The bell four miles out past the loams went at about two in the morning in the night of the twenty-first, for about nine minutes, with the building with two doors open, a piece of paper laid on the table with four legs against its outside wall, and about nine inches of rope inside a beam with a knot in the end of it tied in a right hand. **ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE ARE IN THE RECORD OF THE SECOND NIGHT AND CANNOT BE TAKEN OFF IT. NOBODY DECIDED TO PULL IT AND NOBODY ASKED THE MAN WHO HELD THE ROPE, AND THE REASON FOR THE KNOT IS IN THE BINDING.**
2. **THE POWER CHANGE IS SPENT, AND WHAT HE LOSES IS THE READING.** **THE COLUMN RULED IN THE PUBLIC BOOK FOUR HUNDRED YARDS FROM THAT GATE FOR THE NAME OF WHOEVER READ A THING OUT LOUD IS RULED AND EMPTY AND IS THE SEVENTH OF THAT SHAPE OF THING IN THIS DISTRICT, AND HIS NAME IS NOT IN IT, AND HE IS NOT GOING TO ASK THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THAT BOOK TO PUT IT THERE, AND THE ONE NAME IN CHALK AT THE FOOT OF THE ROLL IS A DIFFERENT COLUMN AND WAS NOT USED AS A SUBSTITUTE FOR IT AND MUST NOT BE.** He asked for nothing and a clerk entered that he asked for nothing, five times across the ten days. **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT PUT HIS NAME IN THAT COLUMN AND MAY NOT HAVE HIM READ A DOCUMENT OUT LOUD IN THIS DISTRICT AGAIN WITHOUT A SCENE THAT EARNS IT AND A COST ENTERED.**
3. **THE NAME IS SPENT, AND IT IS NOT ON THE PAGE.** Said once in a room on the twenty-fifth of the eighth month by the man of about sixty-four to the woman of fifty-eight, and again in a yard on the twenty-sixth. **IT IS THE NAME OF A PERSON WHO IS ONE OF THE FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN, IT IS NOT WRITTEN ON ANY DOCUMENT IN THIS DISTRICT, IT IS NOT IN EITHER OF THE TWO BOOKS, AND NO PLACE AND NO BODY IS NAMED WITH IT. A CLERK MAY ENTER THAT A NAME WAS SAID AND NOT WHAT IT WAS, AND THAT IS ALL A CLERK MAY ENTER. THE CLOSE MUST NOT PUT THE NAME ON A PAGE, IN A FOOTER, IN A CARD, IN A LEDGER, OR IN THE ROLL.**
4. **ONE LINE IS IN THE SECOND OF THE TWO BOOKS, IN HER OWN HAND, AND NOBODY ASKED HER.** Written on the twenty-ninth of the eighth month. **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT WRITE A SECOND LINE INTO THAT BOOK, AND A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT HAVE THAT LINE CORRECTED, AND A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT HAVE IT STRUCK.**
5. **THE COST IS PAID IN TWO BODIES AND IN SCENES.** An ear, lost at about the fourth minute of it and not got back, told in a yard eleven minutes after by the man who named the day. Twenty-two miles in the dark on a hand that will not close, two hours of sleep after six nights without, and nine minutes standing at the end of a table because the yard was too bright after the dark. **NEITHER COST IS IN A FOOTER AND NEITHER IS REVERSIBLE.**

## Still reserved, still untouched, and still not to be closed by a later block without a chapter that spends it on the page and enters what it cost

**The two past pullings, and the two walls a mile apart that cannot both be true, and the man of fifty-six who has now heard that bell three times and been told nothing three times; the office; the fourth line of the offer and the departure; the name of the city on the agent's ledger; the name of the agent in any register in the west bank; the ninth of the nine charters, still empty; the third column of the covenant, still empty; the ninth place on the page out of the cave, still empty; the ninth of the nine printed nights, a hundred and thirty-one days back and named in all forty chapters of this volume and closed in none of them; the shelter's charter and its wrong fifth line; the child of about eight, who was in the sound of the second night and was not asked anything and is in one ledger and in no scene; the boy of nine and his name in a column; the two figures of five years and nine years; the man of about thirty-one whose name is on a page against this district's records office; the man of about forty-four with a case and his list; the man with the portfolio; the man of about forty-one who keeps a road house; the man of about thirty at a counter, who is at the sixteenth time and the next is the seventeenth; the answer to the rival record, which is in force eighty-nine days and unanswered; the second line of the sheet of five lines of print and the job called **COMPLETED BY**; the sheet of print on the top step of two stones; the four empty houses belonging to a woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale; the eleven words, which cannot be found out; the one more name in that night behind the twenty-fourth of the sixth month, which the man of about sixty-four will not say is a name or a hand; and the resolution of the romance. A keeper. Cael Orin, whose name was said once in a yard in Chapter 203. Selik Marne, a wary ally and not a friend. Iven Tallow, the ninth design signature, the origin of the Common Measure, the First House, the removed signature, the blank lot, the three unexplained things of Volume 01, the iron, the echo of an origin toll, the Last Fare, the market stalls on the lower terrace, the first channel's failure and its sills, the four gaps, the two impressions, the two lines holding the office's name, the four lines of the broken circle, the signal's memory pattern, the pan that cannot report itself, the place that cannot report itself, and the mark on the iron. NO NEW FINAL ENEMY. **THE BELL IS NEVER GIVEN ITS NAME IN THE PROSE AND THE VOLUME'S TITLE IS NOT A NAME THE PROSE MAY USE.**

## The three threads this block opened that the next volume must not close by accident

1. **THE NAME IS IN A YARD AND A NAME SAID OUT LOAD IS HANDED TO A BODY THAT IS ALREADY HOLDING A COUNT OF ANSWERERS.** The district now has three numbers about one piece of ground — a number made by knocking, a number made by walking and a number made by listening — and a name, and **THE THREE ARE NOT ADDED TOGETHER AND THE NAME IS NOT A FOURTH OF THEM AND NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS A WAY OF PRICE THE DIFFERENCE.** A man of about thirty-four said so out loud in a yard on the twenty-eighth of the eighth month and a clerk entered that the not being able to price it is not a mystery left dangling and is not a promise about a later day. **A LATER BLOCK THAT PRICES IT HAS SPENT THE NEXT VOLUME'S FIRST MOVE. A LATER BLOCK THAT HANDS THE NAME TO A BODY FOUR HUNDRED MILES OFF HAS DONE THE THING THIS VOLUME WAS AGAINST.**
2. **THE COLUMN FOR THE NAME OF WHOEVER READ A THING OUT LOUD IS RULED AND EMPTY.** **IT IS THE SEVENTH OF THAT SHAPE OF THING IN THIS DISTRICT AND IS NOT THE SAME COLUMN AS ANY OF THE SIX AND IS NOT ADDED TO THEM, AND IT IS NOT FILLED. 3. **THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR IS STILL HERE, WITH NOTHING IN HIS HAND, AND A MAN WHOSE TRADE IS OVER IS NOT A FAILURE AND IS NOT A TRAGIC FIGURE AND IS NOT REDEEMED.** He has been in this district thirty-nine days and slept on that low wall on thirty-nine of the forty nights of that run, he filled two buckets and carried them up a bank on the morning of the twenty-eighth, and a clerk entered that a man staying is not a post and is not a role and is not a bearer of that wall, and that nobody in that yard said anything about him and that that is entered and is not a finding. **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT MAKE HIM A KEEPER, MAY NOT MAKE HIM A BEARER, MAY NOT GIVE HIM A JOB NOBODY ASKED HIM FOR, AND MAY NOT HAVE ANYBODY SAY ANYTHING ABOUT IT IN A YARD.**

## The standing of the third month, and the mechanism a later block may use again

**THE THIRD MONTH IS FIVE MONTHS BACK FROM THE THIRTIETH OF THE EIGHTH MONTH ONWARD AND THE FIGURE IS ON THE PAGE. THE TWO WRONG FIGURES WERE *THREE MONTHS BACK* AND *FOUR MONTHS BACK*, AND THEY APPEAR IN THE TEN CHAPTERS OF THIS BLOCK ONLY ON CHAPTER 344 AND ONLY WHERE THEY ARE BEING NAMED AS WRONG. NO LEDGER PAGE WAS ALTERED AND NO PAGE IS TO BE ALTERED. THE MECHANISM, WHICH IS CHAPTER 311'S OWN, IS: A PERSON SAYS IT OUT LOUD IN A YARD AND NOT IN A ROOM, A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS WRITES IT IN ONE HAND ON ONE LINE WITH *LAST MONTH* AND *THE MONTH BEFORE LAST* IN A COLUMN OF THEIR OWN, THE OLD PAGES ARE LEFT ALONE, AND THE PERSON ASKS FOR IT TO BE ENTERED. A LATER BLOCK MAY USE IT AGAIN AND MUST NAME THE DAY IT CHOSE AND GIVE THE REASON IF THE DAY IS NOT THE ONE A PLAN SUGGESTS.**

## The block's own day-lists, and the standing warning

**EVERY DAY-LIST IN THE CANON CONTRACT AND THE BLOCK RECORD WAS MEASURED, AND THE FIGURES THAT WERE NOT MEASURED ARE PRINTED AS NOT MEASURED. A CARD THAT MEASURES ITS OWN PROSE AND PRINTS THE RESULT IS COMPETING WITH THE NOVEL, AND NOTHING IN THIS REPOSITORY GAINS OR LOSES BY A FIGURE IN ANY CELL. THE META SWEEP OVER THIS BLOCK AND OVER CHAPTER 340 RETURNS ZERO FOR THE WHOLE CLASS, AND CHAPTER 351 DID NOT EXIST AND COULD NOT BE SWEPT, AND VOLUME 07'S SIX HITS WERE NOT REPAIRED BY THIS PHASE AND ARE NOT CLAIMED AS REPAIRED. THE RESERVED LIST OF THIRTY-FOUR TERMS IS AT ZERO ACROSS THE TEN CHAPTERS, MEASURED, AND THE SCAN IS CLEAN BECAUSE NO DOCUMENT SETTLED ANYTHING, AND A NAME A BLOCK PRINTS PUTS A HIT IN THE ONE SCAN IN THIS REPOSITORY THAT CATCHES IT.**

## 16. WHAT THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0003 RAISED, WHAT WAS REPAIRED, AND THE TWO THREADS IT OPENED

**A REVIEW RAN ON THIS BLOCK, FOUND FOUR PROSE DEFECTS, ONE STATE-FILE FORMULA, ONE CARD ROW THAT MISCOUNTED ITS OWN PROSE, AND ONE SELF-AUDIT WHOSE CLEAN RESULT WAS AN ARTEFACT OF ITS OWN PATTERN LIST. ALL SIX ARE CLOSED. IT ALSO FOUND A CRAFT PROBLEM THE BLOCK HAD MEASURED, DISCLOSED, AND CALLED THE VOLUME'S TEXTURE, AND THAT IS THE ONE THAT IS STILL OPEN, AND IT IS THE REASON THIS SECTION EXISTS.**

### 16.1 CLOSED, IN THE PROSE

1. **Nine feet of wire in the fingers of one hand at Chapter 361**, where Chapters 362 and 363 carry nine *inches* of fresh wire in the fingers and the nine feet are a different coil that has already gone into a gate by 363. Corrected to nine inches. **THE FIGURE THE DAY'S WORK IS MEASURED IN DID NOT MOVE AND THE TWELVE FEET IN THE MONTH AT 370 IS UNAFFECTED.**
2. **“ON THIS MORNING” AT CHAPTERS 364 AND 369.** A construction that is wrong twice in ten chapters is a habit. Both corrected.
3. **“A SIXTH OF THE FIVE THINGS THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE” AT CHAPTER 365**, which is not a construction. Rewritten. **“IS NOT A SIXTH OF THE FIVE PROTECTED THINGS” AT 368 IS THE VOLUME PLAN'S OWN IDIOM AND IS LEFT ALONE, AND THAT IS A DISCLOSURE.**
4. **The only frame break in the block, at Chapter 367**, where a ledger entry told a reader that a man was *not in this chapter*. Rewritten. **A REVIEW FOUND IT BY ADDING ONE STRING TO THE BLOCK'S OWN SWEEP LIST, WHICH MEANS THE CLEAN SWEEP RESULT WAS THE VOCABULARY'S FAULT AND NOT THE PROSE'S. THE LIST IS NOW CLOSED AT ONE HUNDRED AND FORTY-THREE PATTERNS AND RETURNS ZERO, AND THE IN-WORLD CLASS IS SPLIT OUT AND PRINTED BESIDE IT.**

### 16.2 CLOSED, IN THE FILES

5. **“596 PLUS THE DAY OF THE TENTH, WHICH GIVES 595 ON THE THIRTIETH OF THE NINTH”** in `state/current.md`. A base that yields 595 for no day, applied to a day of a different month. Corrected: the thirtieth of the ninth is `565 + 30`, and the tenth's base is `596 + g` giving 597 on its first and 604 on its eighth. **`workspace/volume-08/batch-0004/PROMPT.md` HAD 595 AT CHAPTER 371 ALL ALONG AND WAS RIGHT AND THE STATE LINE WAS WRONG.**
6. **“IT IS ENTERED ON ONE DAY ONLY”** in this block's card, for the removal day. Measured: the string is on two, at 365 and 369, at fifty-three and fifty-seven days past. Corrected. **A CARD THAT DESCRIBES THE PROSE IS ITSELF PROSE, AND THIS IS THE FOURTH TIME THIS REPOSITORY HAS HAD TO SAY SO.**

### 16.3 STILL OPEN, AND THE NEXT BLOCK SPENDS IT

- **THE CROSS-CHAPTER FRAME DEBT. 1,092 distinct twelve-word strings appear in more than one of the block's ten chapters, five per cent of all such strings, between 11.6 and 17.9 per cent of each chapter's own windows, twenty-two of them in all ten chapters. They cluster into three standing passages: the opening of that yard with the man of about sixty-four at the foot of the low wall; the light going off that yard at about half past eight with the figure still four hundred and eleven; and the fourth of the five things a document that sets a thing out has to say.** The frame is fixed at *in the ordinary voice* thirty-two, *about nine people* fifty-eight, *about four people* forty-three, *about nineteen people* twenty-nine, *cannot be counted either way* twenty-one. **THE WRITER'S OWN PASS MEASURED THIS AND CALLED IT NOT A DEFECT. A REVIEW FOUND THAT THE MEASUREMENTS DO NOT SUPPORT THE CONCLUSION AND THAT A READER CROSSING A CHAPTER BOUNDARY MEETS THE SAME CLAUSE SKELETONS. IT IS THE DEBT OF `workspace/volume-08/batch-0004/PROMPT.md` SECTION 9 ITEM 5, WHICH IS WRITTEN AS A REQUIREMENT TO WRITE BELOW EACH COUNT AND NOT AS A FOURTH MEASUREMENT.**
- **THE FREEZE IS WRONG ON THE PAGE AND NOT ONLY IN PRINCIPLE. “FIVE MONTHS BACK” IS FROZEN IN `outline/volume-08.md` SECTION 7 AS A FIGURE ABOUT WORDS. `chapter-0366.md` PRESERVES THE HEDGE VERBATIM AND SPEAKS IT ON THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF THE NINTH MONTH, WHERE THE THIRD MONTH IS SIX MONTHS BACK. THE NEXT BLOCK STANDS IN THE TENTH, WHERE THE SAME PHRASE MAKES IT SEVEN. THE CANON IS KEPT AND NO LEDGER PAGE IS ALTERED; WHAT IS OWED IS THE FLAG IN A MOUTH.**

## 18. WHAT THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0004 RAISED, WHAT WAS REPAIRED, WHAT WAS RULED AGAINST, AND WHAT IS STILL OPEN

**A REVIEW RAN ON CHAPTERS 371 TO 380 AND FOUND EIGHT THINGS. FOUR OF ITS EIGHT FINDINGS WERE REAL AND ARE CLOSED, ONE WAS RIGHT ABOUT A DEFECT AND WRONG ABOUT WHICH CHAPTERS CARRY IT, AND THREE OF ITS FIGURE CLAIMS WERE MEASURED AGAINST THE PROSE AND FOUND WRONG, AND ONE WAS A CONTROLLER FILE IT WAS RIGHT ABOUT AND THIS PHASE MUST NOT TOUCH.** The full reasoning, with the method beside every figure, is at `state/volume-08-batch-0004-summary.md` section 5.3. What follows is the standing part.

### 18.1 THE FIGURE-CHECKING RULE THIS REVIEW PROVED AGAIN, AND IT IS THE ONE TO CARRY

**A BLOCK HAS AT LEAST THREE COUNTERS IN IT AND THEY CAN LOOK LIKE ONE.** The removal-day counter, the term-on-that-reading counter and the bid counter all print as *N days* and all three were measured in this block by the same review, and the review took the removal-day rule, which is plain date difference from a named day, and applied it to the term, whose anchor is the day after the thirtieth of the ninth, and to the bid, whose anchor is the first of the ninth. **A COUNTER IS CHECKED AGAINST ITS OWN ANCHOR AND AGAINST AN EARLIER DAY OF THE SAME BLOCK, AND NEVER AGAINST A SIMILAR-LOOKING COUNTER IN ANOTHER FIGURE.** The review's two *correct* figure claims about the prompt were both false positives that would have introduced new errors into a prompt written for the next block.

**AND THE BLOCK'S OWN COUNT TABLE IS PROSE AND IS THE FOURTH TIME A DOCUMENT DESCRIBING THE PROSE HAS BEEN WRONG.** **Seven** rows of the table at `outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0004.md` carried day lists the chapters do not support, and the term row carried the wrong figure as well as the wrong day list. **THE CARD ROW THAT SAID THE REMOVAL DAY WAS ENTERED ON ONE DAY WAS REPAIRED IN THE REVIEW OF BATCH 0003, AND THIS BLOCK REPEATED THE SAME KIND OF ERROR IN SEVEN MORE ROWS.** A day list in a card is a claim about ten chapters and is measured against ten chapters or it is not a figure.

### 18.2 THE STANDING LIST A LATER BLOCK MUST CARRY, BECAUSE THE REVIEW FOUND IT MISSING FROM THE LIVE FILE

- **THE UNTOUCHED LIST IN `state/current.md` IS A STANDING CONSTRAINT AND IT IS NOT SELF-UPDATING.** Two strings left it in this block — *a lot book*, which is the instrument the block was built to rule and the whole of its cost, and *a chair*, which is the block's last act — and the list still said they were at zero. **A LATER BLOCK MAY NOT DISCOVER A SPENT STRING FROM A CONTRADICTION IN THE LIVE FILE. THE LIST IS AMENDED IN PLACE, WITH THE COUNTS AND THE DAYS, WHEN A BLOCK SPENDS A STRING, AND THE AMENDMENT IS THE REPAIR.**
- **A STANDING LIST THAT SAYS *ACROSS THE TEN CHAPTERS THAT HAVE JUST BEEN WRITTEN* IS A LIST WITH A SHELF LIFE OF ONE BLOCK AND IT WAS READ AS A LIST FOR ALL THREE HUNDRED AND EIGHTY.** It now reads as a list across all committed chapters, and the counts in it are per-block measurements with the days named.
- **THE COUNTS IN THAT LIST THAT WERE STALE AND ARE NOW RE-MEASURED: *a market* two, both denials, 371 and 376; *a party* three, all three denials on 376; *keeper* seven, all seven denials; *a lot book* fifteen across eight days; *a chair* fourteen on 380, and *a chair is a chair* two on 380. *a rate* is at one, on 366, in Batch 0003, and at zero in this block.**

### 18.3 THE ONE FINDING THAT IS NOT CLOSED, AND IT IS NOT OURS TO CLOSE

**`state/phase-ledger.json` STILL READS `currentPhase: phase-000-bootstrap`, `status: planned`, `attempts: 0`, `range: null`, WITH THREE HUNDRED AND EIGHTY CHAPTERS COMMITTED AND EIGHT BATCHES OF VOLUME 08 WRITTEN. THE REVIEW IS RIGHT AND IT IS NOT THIS PHASE'S FILE.** It is listed in the instructions as controller-owned, and the workflow owns phase selection, status and attempts. **RECORDED HERE SO THAT IT IS NOT LOST, AND DELIBERATELY NOT EDITED. IF A WRITER FINDS THE LEDGER IN THIS STATE AGAIN, THE ACTION IS TO REPORT IT AND NOT TO REPAIR IT, BECAUSE A WRITER THAT WRITES ITS OWN PHASE STATUS CANNOT BE COUNTED AS HAVING REACHED A PHASE.**

### 18.4 THE CRAFT DEBT, RESTATED AS AN OBLIGATION AND NOT AS A COUNT

The cross-chapter frame debt is at section 17.3 and the counts do not reconcile across three methods, and the method is not printed for the largest pair of them. **The obligation does not depend on the count: the frame of a sentence in this volume has been carrying clause skeletons across chapter boundaries for four blocks, and the next block owes variation in the frame itself.** Measuring it a fourth time, printing the fall, and calling the fall a clearance is what produced this entry, and the entry is the reason not to do it again.

## 19. THE STATE OF EVERY THREAD AT THE END OF VOLUME 08, THE EIGHTEENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH, AFTER BATCH 0005, AND WHAT THE CLOSE OWES

**THE BLOCK THAT HAS JUST BEEN WRITTEN IS THE FIFTH AND LAST BLOCK OF CHAPTERS IN VOLUME 08. ITS RECORD IS `state/volume-08-batch-0005-summary.md` AND ITS CANON CONTRACT IS `outline/batches/volume-08-batch-0005.md`. NOTHING BELOW IS NEW CANON AND EVERY FIGURE WAS MEASURED AFTER THE LAST PROSE EDIT.**

### 19.1 THE THREADS THIS BLOCK SPENT, AND WHAT IS LEFT OF EACH

1. **The reversal: SPENT, AND RESOLVED BY A RULE AND NOT BY A POST.** The whole of the binding rested on a second person reading a thing back from the top, and this district had one, and he was gone four days in a week. **A RULE ABOUT WHAT MAKES A THING BIND WAS SAID OUT LOUD IN THE YARD OF LOT SEVENTEEN ON THE TENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH, IN TWO HUNDRED AND TWO WORDS, WITH NOBODY'S NAME IN IT, AND ITS PRICE WAS SAID OUT LOUD AFTER IT IN THE SAME VOICE, AND THE PRICE IS THAT NOBODY CAN BE MADE TO KEEP IT. A CLERK ENTERED THE RULE ON A PAGE. NO SHEET THIS VOLUME MAKES CARRIES IT. NO NAME WENT INTO ANY COLUMN.**
2. **The cost of the reversal, COLLECTED ON THE PAGE.** On the thirteenth of the tenth month the man who convened the first reading was not the man who read it, and the toll was said out loud, and nobody read any of it back from the top. **THE ONLY PERSON WHO HAD EVER READ A THING BACK FROM THE TOP HAD SAID OUT LOUD ON THE ELEVENTH OF THAT MONTH THAT SHE WAS NOT GOING TO DO IT AGAIN, AND NOBODY ASKED HER TO, AND THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT ASKING SAYS NOT ASKED.** What is left: a district that can say the rule and cannot make anybody keep it, and that is the standing condition of the volume and not a fault in it.
3. **A second reader: SPENT, AND IT IS NOBODY IN PARTICULAR.** Said out loud at Chapter 382 and refused a post out loud at Chapter 382 and again at Chapter 390. The man of about nineteen was told in a yard, with a reason, that he is not the second reader, and was not asked for it. **NOBODY WAS APPOINTED, NO DOCUMENT NAMES A JOB THAT DOES NOT EXIST, AND THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-NINE WHO DRIVES A CART CAME INTO THAT YARD ONCE IN THIS BLOCK, ON THE SEVENTEENTH OF THE TENTH, AND READ NOTHING, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM ANYTHING.** What is left: nothing to appoint and nobody to appoint, which is the resolution.
4. **The third of the three things a bell needs before this district will call a sounding safe: SPENT, ON THE ELEVENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH, IN A SCENE, WITH A COST.** True of a building with two doors held for a term of a month on the two terms lying on the table against its outside wall, and of a way of getting a thing read out and read back from the top by a second person who is nobody in particular. **THE WORD THE CANON KEEPS AT ZERO WAS NOT USED, THE THINGS ARE NAMED IN THE PROSE AS THE THREE THIS DISTRICT HAS TO HAVE BEFORE IT WILL CALL A SOUNDING SAFE, AND NOT ONE LINE OF THE THREE IS PRINTED ON ANY SHEET THIS DISTRICT MAKES.** What is left: the third of the three is on a page, and a thing on a page is not a thing that has been done, and the earliest day the thing could be pulled again went past unused on the twenty-first of the ninth in the second movement of this volume and nobody in this district said a word about it then and nobody has said a word about it since.
5. **A woman of fifty-eight asked to read a thing back from the top: SPENT, AND NOT HONOURED.** Asked out loud, in that yard, in daylight, in front of about nineteen people. She said one thing about nineteen years and then read the line back from the top and got it right. **NOBODY IN THAT YARD SAID ONE WORD ABOUT THE NINETEEN YEARS, A CLERK DID NOT ENTER THAT SHE WAS HONOURED, AND A CLERK MAY NOT ENTER IT. SHE SAID OUT LOUD AFTERWARDS THAT SHE IS NOT GOING TO DO IT AGAIN AND THAT NOBODY HAS ASKED HER TO DO IT AGAIN.** What is left: her refusal of the next time, which is a refusal and is not a refusal of the district, and the fact that she asked a man for a day's work on the fifteenth and was not thanked for that either.
6. **A right hand that stopped closing in the middle of a reading: SPENT, ON THE TWELFTH OF THE TENTH MONTH.** It opened about four inches in the middle of the sentence about the toll, the sheet went into the wet boards, and a lot went under the mender's forearm for about eleven minutes. **THE READING WAS BEGUN TWICE AND FINISHED ONCE, AND THE MAN WHO CONVENED IT IS NOT THE MAN WHO READ IT, AND THE COLUMN FOR THE NAME OF WHOEVER READ A THING OUT LOUD IS RULED AND WAS EMPTY AT ABOUT SIX AND NOTHING WENT INTO IT BY ANYBODY.** What is left: the hand. It has not closed properly since the tenth of the eighth month and it does not close now.
7. **A toll and what a holder owes under a term: SPENT, ON THE THIRTEENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH.** A toll is a thing a holder pays under a term; there is no holder of that lot and there never has been; if a building were a holder the toll would fall on whoever was standing inside it; and a building cannot be asked who is in it. **A TOLL IS AT THIRTEEN ON THE PAGE ACROSS THE BLOCK AND NINE OF THOSE THIRTEEN ARE ON THAT ONE DAY.** What is left: the fifth of the five things this district does not have, which is a way to pay a person who is not in a household, which is still not paid and whose count is still five.
8. **The bank, the door, and one question: SPENT, ON THE FOURTEENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH.** He climbed about nine feet of bank, stood four minutes out of breath on a step of two stones, did not knock, she opened the door, he asked one question, she said no in about four seconds with a reason of her own, he said thank you and did not ask twice. **NEITHER OF THEM SAID A DECLARATION, THE ONE LINE IN THE SECOND OF THE TWO BOOKS WAS NOT READ ALOUD BY ANYBODY, HE WAS NOT TOLD WHAT IT SAYS, AND A CLERK OF NINETEEN YEARS DID NOT GO UP THAT BANK ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS OF THIS BLOCK, WHICH IS A CHANGE FROM THE PREVIOUS BLOCK AND WAS NOT ASKED FOR.** What is left: the romance, unresolved, and a door that was opened and is not a consent to anything.
9. **A day's work and what it is worth: SPENT, ON THE FIFTEENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH.** Asked for out loud in a yard by the woman of fifty-eight, done by a man of about thirty-seven who cuts reeds up to his waist, not paid for, and refused a figure out loud before he was asked for one. What is left: the fifth of the five, unpaid.
10. **Five different ninths on one page: SPENT, ON THE SIXTEENTH OF THE TENTH MONTH.** Five headed empty columns and four columns of not-askings, nine columns, nothing written in any of them, and a sixth column of the same shape refused out loud in about four seconds. What is left: five open ninths, none of them closed, and a boy who copied them and got the third one wrong and left the word out on purpose.

# Section 25 — VOLUME 09 BATCH 0003 — OPEN THREADS AFTER CHAPTERS 411 TO 420, WHAT THIS BLOCK OPENED, SPENT, AND LEFT ALONE

**THE BLOCK IS CHAPTERS 411 TO 420, THE NINTH TO THE EIGHTEENTH OF THE ELEVENTH MONTH, AND IT IS THE MOVEMENT THE VOLUME'S MIDPOINT REVERSAL BELONGS TO. IT RESOLVED NOTHING.**

## 25.1 THE THREAD THE WHOLE VOLUME IS ABOUT, AND WHAT THESE TEN DAYS DID TO IT

**THE FIGURE ON THE SECOND LINE OF THAT LOT BOOK IS A MONTH OLD AS OF THE FIFTEENTH OF THE ELEVENTH, AND IT IS STILL ON THE FACE OF A BOOK A STRANGER MAY WALK UP TO AND READ, AND IT IS NOT CORRECTED, AND EVERY PERSON IN THAT YARD NOW KNOWS IT.** Thirty was said out loud in that yard at about ten past nine on the fifteenth, a clerk entered it in the morning with the day it stopped being true not counted in, the man who put it there said out loud that he put it there, the man whose mouth it came out of said so too, and about four people wanted the day put on the page and it was refused out loud for a reason that was not that the idea is wrong. **THE THREAD IS FURTHER ALONG THAN IT WAS AND IT IS EXACTLY WHERE IT WAS.** The reversal at Chapter 415 named the pressure for the first time and moved it nowhere.

## 25.2 THE THREADS THIS BLOCK TOUCHED WITHOUT SPENDING, AND THE WORDS FOR EACH

- **The reading of that lot.** Begun and not finished, at the fourth of the five, stopped three times. **Not advanced on any of these ten days, and the fifth of the five is at zero across them, measured.** The mender and the man of about thirty-four are the only two people in this district who could be named in a remedy and neither is going to be asked.
- **The bid.** Open sixty-nine days rising to seventy-eight, not run on one of the ten, nothing proposed about closing it on any of them in a mouth or in a page. **A NEW WORD CAME OFF IT AND THE WORD IS THIS: a man of fifty-six said out loud at Chapter 415 that the number of days a bid has stood open is not a figure about the bid, it is a figure about a page, and that nobody has run a bid in this district in six months and there is no second occasion in this basin to compare it with. THAT IS A FINDING ABOUT THE BID AND IT IS NOT AN ANSWER TO IT AND IT MAY NOT BE RE-AIMED AS EITHER.**
- **The rule said out loud in that yard on the tenth of the tenth month.** Thirty-eight days standing at the end of this block, a figure about a rule and not about how many days anybody kept it. **At Chapter 420 a man was asked to say the price of it out loud again and said no and asked the clerk to enter that he did not say it, and the record about the not asking says not asked.** The price is unspent and is not to be softened into a difficulty.
- **The second of the two books four hundred yards up that bank.** One line, in her own hand, nobody asked her. **SEALED AND UNTOUCHED. NOBODY WENT UP THAT BANK ON ANY OF THESE TEN DAYS, ENTERED AT CHAPTER 420.**
- **The man of about thirty-seven who cuts reeds.** The only person who knows the day the figure stopped being true. In that yard at about half past nine on the sixteenth, at the end of that table at about four on the eleventh, and at the top of that bank with his back to the yard for about four minutes on the sixteenth. **HE SAID FIVE WORDS IN SIX WEEKS AND WAS NOT ASKED FOR A SIXTH, AND HE WAS NOT ASKED FOR ANYTHING ON ANY OF THE TEN DAYS, AND THE RECORD ABOUT THE NOT ASKING SAYS NOT ASKED ON ALL THREE DAYS HE IS IN THAT YARD.**
- **The figure off a list.** Ten days old rising to nineteen, entered on the thirtieth of the tenth month off a list in a room four hundred yards up that bank, with the word *unchecked* beside it. **It is the one figure in this block that nobody can check, and it is the figure the whole reversal is about, and the two of those are two figures and are not added together. It was not checked on any of the ten days and its check is Chapter 433's.**
- **The word *unchecked* on twelve figures in a clerk's margin.** On the page on all ten days. **Said out loud in mouths at Chapter 411 by the clerk who wrote it, and at Chapter 412 by the man of about thirty-seven who puts tables up, and at Chapter 413 and 419 it is the thing people argue about rather than the thing anybody reads.** It is over nothing and it is not over any column and no fifth column of any shape was ruled.
- **The column for the name of whoever read a thing out loud.** Named on all ten days and empty at about six on all ten. **The column was asked for out loud at Chapter 415 and refused in about four seconds, and a boy of about nineteen said out loud at Chapter 414 that he was not asking for one and that if anybody wrote his not asking on a page they would have taken the word not out of it.** RULED AND STILL EMPTY.
- **The two walls a mile apart.** *The two walls*, *wall a mile* and *walls a mile* at zero across the ten days, measured. **UNTOUCHED AND OPEN, AND THE REVERSAL DID NOT TOUCH IT.** A low wall about four feet high at the south end of that yard is in-world and is the block's last image.
- **The ninth of the nine printed nights.** A hundred and two days back rising to two hundred and eleven. **NAMED ON THREE OF THE TEN DAYS, 411, 414 AND 417, MEASURED ONE DAY AT A TIME, AND CLOSED IN NONE OF THEM.** No bell was pulled on any of the ten days and no night was named on seven of them, and Chapters 412 and 416 say so in their own sentences.
- **The old shelter's charter.** Wrong on its face, in a book a stranger may walk up to, since Volume 07. **At zero across these ten days, measured.** Not corrected, not replaced, not superseded, not taken out.
- **The man of about sixty-four.** A hundred and ten days in rising to a hundred and nineteen, a hundred and nine nights of a hundred and ten rising to a hundred and eighteen of a hundred and nineteen, nothing in his hands, given nothing. **On Chapter 413 he got up off the stones at the foot of that low wall and sat down again about two feet along, and nobody offered him a hand, and a clerk entered that nothing was asked and nothing was given.** He is not a keeper of a lot, not a bearer of the reading, not the second reader, not a post, not a steward and not a job nobody asked him for. **A CLERK IS NOT GOING TO SAY WHAT HE IS GOING TO DO WITH HIS HANDS BECAUSE NOBODY ASKED HER, AND A CLERK DID NOT SAY THAT A MAN WHOSE TRADE IS OVER IS A FAILURE AND DID NOT SAY THAT HE IS NOT ONE EITHER.**
- **The protagonist's name.** Not printed, not said, not asked for, not on any page, column, book, sheet, sign or mark. **NOBODY IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS CALLS HIM ANYTHING AT ALL.**
- **The four empty houses of the woman of about thirty-six who keeps a scale.** Four figures about four houses and not a figure about her. **On Chapter 412 a man said out loud that he had heard she had four houses and was not asking her about them, and she stopped and said that the thing under her arm was a blanket, and on Chapter 420 she stopped at that table for about four seconds and read the two lines and went on, and a man at the east wall said out loud that he was not going to say whether that was about the book and neither was anybody else.** NOT RESOLVED AND NOT TO BE RESOLVED BY ACCIDENT.
- **The tent rolled on a hand-cart.** Named at the east wall on the last four of these ten days and not resolved. **He put both hands either side of the book on Chapter 415 and did not touch it, and said a figure of nine hundred out loud that nobody could check including himself, and nobody thanked him and he was given nothing.**
- **A body four hundred miles off that prints at an interval.** Thirty-eight days past a printing it did not make rising to forty-seven. **At Chapter 420 a person in that yard said out loud that nobody has looked for it and that they are not going to say anything else about it, and that a clerk is not going to enter that anybody was waiting for it.** It has no face. It did not acquire one.

## 25.5 THE TWO THINGS A WRITER INHERITS AS THINGS TO LOOK FOR, NOT AS THINGS TO REPEAT

**ONE: A DAY-LIST IN A DOCUMENT IS A FINDING AND NOT A TRANSCRIPTION.** The list for the ninth of the nine printed nights in the phase prompt for this block was checked against the ten chapter files, one day at a time, and it is **three days, 411, 414 and 417**, and the string is on five. The figure off a list in the card for Chapter 411 was **nine days old** and the figure on the ninth of the eleventh is **ten days old**, because the entry day is counted as the first, and the card is corrected in the canon card. **AND ONE ARITHMETIC ERROR IN THE PHASE PROMPT'S OWN SENTENCE ON THE TRAIN WAS FOUND AND IS CORRECTED IN THE BLOCK RECORD AND IN THE CANON CARD RATHER THAN ADOPTED.**

**TWO: A SCRIPT THAT SHORTENS OR SPLITS A SENTENCE DELETES A CONNECTIVE, AND EVERY LINE IT TOUCHED MUST BE READ.** The block before this one ran a mechanical sentence-splitter and it broke the sentences at one hundred and sixteen places. **THIS BLOCK RAN NO SENTENCE-SPLITTER AND NO SCRIPT OF ANY KIND OVER ITS OWN SENTENCES.** Every long sentence in these ten chapters was broken by hand, at a clause boundary a person chose. Where a script was used at all, it applied explicit hand-written before-and-after pairs, one at a time, and it was run four times and every miss it returned on the second, third and fourth runs was a pair already applied or an anchor a previous run had changed, and every one of those was read and resolved by hand before anything was measured. **THE FIGURES THAT CAME OUT OF THAT ARE A COUNT AND NOT A VERDICT, AND THE MEAN AND MEDIAN OF THIS BLOCK'S SENTENCES ARE PRINTED BESIDE BATCH 0002's AND BATCH 0001's SO THAT A CLOSE CAN CHECK ALL THREE.**

### 26.4 THE THREAD NOBODY HAS OWNED, WHICH IS A CONFLICT AND NOT A THREAD, AND IT IS OPEN

**THE WORD *UNCHECKED*.** `outline/volume-09.md` sections 2.4 and 10.1.2 make it the volume's first real finding and make it mandatory on the twelve arms that cross from the tenth month into the eleventh, and say in terms that a block which finds a crossing ladder without the word on it has found a defect. **Chapter 404 carries the word on the arms. Chapters 411 to 420 do not: the word is at eleven across the ten days, all eleven in the margin of a clerk's page, and Chapters 414 and 418 say in their own sentences that it is over nothing and is not over any of the nine columns.** The twelve arms are the board, the train on the siding, the unentered days, the days from the second of January, the pool, the ninth of the nine printed nights, the six households, the days with no line on a board, the rival record, the age of the figure about the figure, the mornings that table has stood, and the removal day, and all twelve do cross inside this volume.

**Nothing is broken and no figure is wrong. What is broken is the record: three blocks of state files set the inversion down in the voice of a thing that was always so, while the volume outline says the opposite, and no file in this repository said the two things disagreed.** `outline/volume-09.md` is the outline phase's file and was not edited by this review. **The two available routes are both legitimate. CARRY THE TWELVE ARMS WITH THE WORD ON THEM, which restores the outline and puts *unchecked* back on a page a stranger can walk up to. OR CARRY THE INVERSION, which is the better image and is what the last three blocks have actually been writing, in which case the volume close owes one sentence saying that the volume's own finding came back empty, and that is a real ending and not a repaired one.** What no later block may do is leave it unrecorded again, which is the actual defect and the only thing in this entry that anybody did wrong.

### 27.3 THE CONFLICT THE FIFTH BLOCK HAS TO KNOW ABOUT, AND WHAT IT COSTS

**THE WORD *UNCHECKED*.** `outline/volume-09.md` sections 2.4 and 10.1.2 make it the volume's first real finding and make it mandatory on the twelve arms that cross the month boundary, and say in terms that a block which finds a crossing ladder without the word on it has found a defect. **Chapter 404 carries the word on the arms.
